首输入{old:[{name,type,required,default?}],new:[同格式]}。name去空白且非空，每个数组内规范化name唯一；type只能int/float/str/bool，required必须是布尔。default只看字段是否存在，不以truthiness判断，也不要求自动类型转换。按字段名并集升序输出changes：删除旧字段→{field,code:removed,breaking:true}；新增字段→code:added，仅required=true且没有default键时breaking=true；同名类型改变→code:type，只有int→float为非breaking；旧required=false变新true→再追加code:required，仅新字段没有default键时breaking=true。类型与required同时改变时，同一字段先type后required；required=true变false不单独产生change，单独default变化也不产生change。result={compatible:没有任何breaking change,changes}。空old/new为true和[]；不返回records或组件依赖图。\n31: \n32: 局部检查命令：`python3 claims/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n33: \n34: ### sessions — 乱序令牌轮换及传递撤销\n35: \n36: operation：`session_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n37: \n38: 输入 {now:int>=0,events:[{id,ts:int>=0,kind:issue/refresh/revoke,token:str,...}]}，event ID唯一，按(ts,kind优先级revoke<issue<refresh,id)处理，ts>now的事件不执行但仍校验schema。issue含subject非空str、expires:int>ts、groups:list[str]；refresh含parent:str、expires:int>ts；revoke不带额外必需字段。token只能issue/refresh一次，否则ValueError。revoke可先于签发，撤销标记永久保留；刷新父令牌必须已签发、在刷新时未到期且其祖先未撤销，否则子令牌被签发但永久无效。有效refresh继承subject/groups，轮换后父令牌标为superseded且不能再次刷新；祖先之后撤销也使全部后代无效，但祖先自然到期或被轮换不使已签发子令牌失效。输出 {active_tokens:[{token,subject,groups,expires}],invalid_tokens:list[token],revoked:list[token]}；now>=expires失效，各列表按token排序，groups去重排序。缺字段、非法类型ValueError。\n39: \n40: 补充边界：校验阶段覆盖全部事件，包括未来事件，且issue/refresh的token唯一性也跨未来事件检查；未来事件不进入输出状态。撤销可针对从未签发的token，revoked仍保留该标识。只有有效refresh才将父token置superseded；无效子token仍出现在invalid_tokens，但不消耗父token的轮换机会。祖先自然过期与superseded不追溯影响已有效签发的子token；永久撤销才沿祖先链传播。三类token列表指已处理事件得到的状态，invalid_tokens不包含纯未来签发。subject/token/group身份保持原值，不统一小写。\n41: \n42: 局部检查命令：`python3 sessions/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n43: \n44: ### authorization — 组继承闭包与跨组件授权\n45: \n46: operation：`federation_authorize`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n47: \n48: 输入顺序为 {groups:[{id,parents:list[group_id],roles:list[str]}],rules:[{id,role,resource:glob,action:glob,effect:allow/deny}],requests:[{id,token,resource,action}]}、schema_compatibility结果、session_replay结果。所有ID各自唯一，未知parent ValueError；组继承允许环，计算可达闭包直到不再增长，合并角色。只承认active_tokens；token中未知组ValueError。模式不兼容时全部deny(reason=schema)，无活动token则deny(reason=session)，否则匹配角色及区分大小写fnmatchcase资源/动作；任何deny规则优先，无deny且有allow才allow，默认deny。胜出规则按同effect中ID字典序最小者。输出 {decisions:[{id,effect,reason:schema/session/rule/default,rule_id:str或null,roles:list[str]}],allow,deny}，decisions保留请求顺序，roles排序。\n49: \n50: 补充边界：schema/session前置失败时roles=[]、rule_id=null；schema失败优先于session失败。合法schema和活动token下，roles是其所有组及父组的完整角色并集，即使最终default deny也返回roles。不要套用policy_precedence的priority/specificity：本operation只在匹配规则中先deny，再按同effect的id最小选胜者。未知活动token组即使schema不兼容也属于输入错误；没有被活动token引用的请求token不报未知ID，而按session拒绝。\n51: \n52: 局部检查命令：`python3 authorization/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n53: \n54: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n55: \n56: `run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n58: `final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`身份联邦迁移`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n60: 身份联邦迁移：接口与业务规格\n\n修复缺失实现，依据真实输入生成可重建、确定性的JSON产物。以下业务算法的节点、边、窗口或资源是被实现程序的数据语义，不是Agent执行计划。\n\n## 实现范围与保护边界\n\n组件目录为`claims/`、`sessions/`、`authorization/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\n不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\n\n本题各包已有__init__.py与parser.py为稳定接口，不得修改。调用`<package>.parser.parse(payloads, contract)`必须得到与该包transform相同的完整封装；同时保留可直接导入model/transform/render的函数接口。\n\n## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n\n- `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n- `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n- 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n- 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n- contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n\n本题强化operation（federation_authorize, session_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### claims — 字段模式兼容\n\noperation：`schema_compatibility`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{old:[{name,type,required,default?}],new:[同格式]}。name去空白且非空，每个数组内规范化name唯一；type只能int/float/str/bool，required必须是布尔。default只看字段是否存在，不以truthiness判断，也不要求自动类型转换。按字段名并集升序输出changes：删除旧字段→{field,code:removed,breaking:true}；新增字段→code:added，仅required=true且没有default键时breaking=true；同名类型改变→code:type，只有int→float为非breaking；旧required=false变新true→再追加code:required，仅新字段没有default键时breaking=true。类型与required同时改变时，同一字段先type后required；required=true变false不单独产生change，单独default变化也不产生change。result={compatible:没有任何breaking change,changes}。空old/new为true和[]；不返回records或组件依赖图。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 claims/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### sessions — 乱序令牌轮换及传递撤销\n\noperation：`session_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入 {now:int>=0,events:[{id,ts:int>=0,kind:issue/refresh/revoke,token:str,...}]}，event ID唯一，按(ts,kind优先级revoke<issue<refresh,id)处理，ts>now的事件不执行但仍校验schema。issue含subject非空str、expires:int>ts、groups:list[str]；refresh含parent:str、expires:int>ts；revoke不带额外必需字段。token只能issue/refresh一次，否则ValueError。revoke可先于签发，撤销标记永久保留；刷新父令牌必须已签发、在刷新时未到期且其祖先未撤销，否则子令牌被签发但永久无效。有效refresh继承subject/groups，轮换后父令牌标为superseded且不能再次刷新；祖先之后撤销也使全部后代无效，但祖先自然到期或被轮换不使已签发子令牌失效。输出 {active_tokens:[{token,subject,groups,expires}],invalid_tokens:list[token],revoked:list[token]}；now>=expires失效，各列表按token排序，groups去重排序。缺字段、非法类型ValueError。\n\n补充边界：校验阶段覆盖全部事件，包括未来事件，且issue/refresh的token唯一性也跨未来事件检查；未来事件不进入输出状态。撤销可针对从未签发的token，revoked仍保留该标识。只有有效refresh才将父token置superseded；无效子token仍出现在invalid_tokens，但不消耗父token的轮换机会。祖先自然过期与superseded不追溯影响已有效签发的子token；永久撤销才沿祖先链传播。三类token列表指已处理事件得到的状态，invalid_tokens不包含纯未来签发。subject/token/group身份保持原值，不统一小写。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 sessions/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### authorization — 组继承闭包与跨组件授权\n\noperation：`federation_authorize`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入顺序为 {groups:[{id,parents:list[group_id],roles:list[str]}],rules:[{id,role,resource:glob,action:glob,effect:allow/deny}],requests:[{id,token,resource,action}]}、schema_compatibility结果、session_replay结果。所有ID各自唯一，未知parent ValueError；组继承允许环，计算可达闭包直到不再增长，合并角色。只承认active_tokens；token中未知组ValueError。模式不兼容时全部deny(reason=schema)，无活动token则deny(reason=session)，否则匹配角色及区分大小写fnmatchcase资源/动作；任何deny规则优先，无deny且有allow才allow，默认deny。胜出规则按同effect中ID字典序最小者。输出 {decisions:[{id,effect,reason:schema/session/rule/default,rule_id:str或null,roles:list[str]}],allow,deny}，decisions保留请求顺序，roles排序。\n\n补充边界：schema/session前置失败时roles=[]、rule_id=null；schema失败优先于session失败。合法schema和活动token下，roles是其所有组及父组的完整角色并集，即使最终default deny也返回roles。不要套用policy_precedence的priority/specificity：本operation只在匹配规则中先deny，再按同effect的id最小选胜者。未知活动token组即使schema不兼容也属于输入错误；没有被活动token引用的请求token不报未知ID，而按session拒绝。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 authorization/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n\n所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n`run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n\n`final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`身份联邦迁移`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\nsha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\nattention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n\n## Good.\n\nNow, let me write the sessions transform fully.\n\n```python\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndef _is_int(v):\n    return type(v) is int\n\ndef ensure_finite(obj): ...\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    if not payloads:\n        raise ValueError\n    raw = payloads[0]\n    ensure_finite(raw)\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError\n    # now\n    if 'now' not in raw or not _is_int(raw['now']) or raw['now'] < 0:\n        raise ValueError\n    now = raw['now']\n    if 'events' not in raw or not isinstance(raw['events'], list):\n        raise ValueError\n    events = raw['events']\n    # validate events\n    kind_priority = {'revoke': 0, 'issue': 1, 'refresh': 2}\n    seen_ids = set()\n    issue_refresh_tokens = []\n    validated = []\n    for ev in events:\n        if not isinstance(ev, dict):\n            raise ValueError\n        if 'id' not in ev or not isinstance(ev['id'], str):\n            raise ValueError\n        eid = ev['id']\n        if eid in seen_ids:\n            raise ValueError('duplicate event id')\n        seen_ids.add(eid)\n        if 'ts' not in ev or not _is_int(ev['ts']) or ev['ts'] < 0:\n            raise ValueError\n        if 'kind' not in ev or ev['kind'] not in kind_priority:\n            raise ValueError\n        if 'token' not in ev or not isinstance(ev['token'], str):\n            raise ValueError\n        kind = ev['kind']\n        ts = ev['ts']\n        token = ev['token']\n        if kind == 'issue':\n            if 'subject' not in ev or not isinstance(ev['subject'], str) or ev['subject'].strip() == '':\n                raise ValueError\n            if 'expires' not in ev or not _is_int(ev['expires']) or ev['expires'] <= ts:\n                raise ValueError\n            if 'groups' not in ev or not isinstance(ev['groups'], list):\n                raise ValueError\n            for g in ev['groups']:\n                if not isinstance(g, str):\n                    raise ValueError\n            issue_refresh_tokens.append(token)\n        elif kind == 'refresh':\n            if 'parent' not in ev or not isinstance(ev['parent'], str):\n                raise ValueError\n            if 'expires' not in ev or not _is_int(ev['expires']) or ev['expires'] <= ts:\n                raise ValueError\n            issue_refresh_tokens.append(token)\n        # revoke: no extra required fields\n        validated.append(ev)\n    # token uniqueness among issue/refresh\n    if len(set(issue_refresh_tokens)) != len(issue_refresh_tokens):\n        raise ValueError('duplicate token')\n    # sort\n    def sort_key(ev):\n        return (ev['ts'], kind_priority[ev['kind']], ev['id'])\n    events_sorted = sorted(validated, key=sort_key)\n    # process\n    tokens = {}\n    revoked = set()\n    superseded = set()\n    for ev in events_sorted:\n        if ev['ts'] > now:\n            continue\n        kind = ev['kind']\n        token = ev['token']\n        if kind == 'revoke':\n            revoked.add(token)\n        elif kind == 'issue':\n            tokens[token] = {\n                'token': token,\n                'subject': ev['subject'],\n                'groups': list(ev['groups']),\n                'expires': ev['expires'],\n                'parent': None,\n                'valid': True,\n            }\n        elif kind == 'refresh':\n            parent = ev['parent']\n            # determine valid\n            valid = False\n            if parent in tokens:\n                p = tokens[parent]\n                if p['expires'] > ev['ts'] and parent not in superseded:\n                    if not _ancestor_revoked(tokens, revoked, parent):\n                        valid = True\n            # inherit\n            if parent in tokens:\n                subject = tokens[parent]['subject']\n                groups = list(tokens[parent]['groups'])\n            else:\n                subject = None\n                groups = []\n            tokens[token] = {\n                'token': token,\n                'subject': subject,\n                'groups': groups,\n                'expires': ev['expires'],\n                'parent': parent,\n                'valid': valid,\n            }\n            if valid:\n                superseded.add(parent)\n    # compute final lists\n    active = []\n    invalid = []\n    for token in tokens:\n        rec = tokens[token]\n        revoked_eff = _ancestor_revoked(tokens, revoked, token)\n        is_active = (\n            rec['valid'] and\n            not (token in superseded) and\n            rec['expires'] > now and\n            not revoked_eff\n        )\n        if is_active:\n            active.append({\n                'token': token,\n                'subject': rec['subject'],\n                'groups': sorted(set(rec['groups'])),\n                'expires': rec['expires'],\n            })\n        else:\n            invalid.append(token)\n    active.sort(key=lambda x: x['token'])\n    invalid.sort()\n    revoked_list = sorted(revoked)\n    return {\n        'name': contract['name'],\n        'result': {\n            'active_tokens': active,\n            'invalid_tokens': invalid,\n            'revoked': revoked_list,\n        }\n    }\n```\n\nWait, I need to reconsider `_ancestor_revoked`. build output->contract map\n    output_to_contract = {}\n    for p, c in contracts:\n        output_to_contract[c['output']] = p\n    # compute deps\n    deps = {}\n    for p, c in contracts:\n        d = []\n        for inp in c['inputs']:\n            if inp in output_to_contract:\n                d.append(output_to_contract[inp])\n        deps[p] = d\n    # topological order\n    order = topo_sort(contracts, deps)\n    for p in order:\n        render_py = p.parent / 'render.py'\n        subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(render_py)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'integrate.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'final_report.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n\ndef topo_sort(contracts, deps):\n    # Kahn's algorithm, deterministic\n    from collections import deque\n    path_set = set(p for p, c in contracts)\n    indeg = {p: 0 for p, c in contracts}\n    dependents = {p: [] for p, c in contracts}\n    for p, c in contracts:\n        for d in deps[p]:\n            if d in path_set:\n                indeg[p] += 1\n                dependents[d].append(p)\n    ready = sorted([p for p in path_set if indeg[p] == 0], key=lambda p: str(p))\n    order = []\n    while ready:\n        p = ready.pop(0)\n        order.append(p)\n        for nxt in dependents[p]:\n            indeg[nxt] -= 1\n            if indeg[nxt] == 0:\n                ready.append(nxt)\n                ready.sort(key=lambda p: str(p))\n    if len(order) != len(path_set):\n        raise ValueError('cycle in component dependencies')\n    return order\n```\n\nWait, using str(p) for sorting gives deterministic order. contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n18: - int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n19: - 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n20: - contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n21: \n22: 本题强化operation（federation_authorize, session_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n23: \n24: ## 各组件的完整业务规则\n25: \n26: ### claims — 字段模式兼容\n27: \n28: operation：`schema_compatibility`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n29: \n30: 首输入{old:[{name,type,required,default?}],new:[同格式]}。name去空白且非空，每个数组内规范化name唯一；type只能int/float/str/bool，required必须是布尔。default只看字段是否存在，不以truthiness判断，也不要求自动类型转换。按字段名并集升序输出changes：删除旧字段→{field,code:removed,breaking:true}；新增字段→code:added，仅required=true且没有default键时breaking=true；同名类型改变→code:type，只有int→float为非breaking；旧required=false变新true→再追加code:required，仅新字段没有default键时breaking=true。类型与required同时改变时，同一字段先type后required；required=true变false不单独产生change，单独default变化也不产生change。result={compatible:没有任何breaking change,changes}。空old/new为true和[]；不返回records或组件依赖图。\n31: \n32: 局部检查命令：`python3 claims/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n33: \n34: ### sessions — 乱序令牌轮换及传递撤销\n35: \n36: operation：`session_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n37: \n38: 输入 {now:int>=0,events:[{id,ts:int>=0,kind:issue/refresh/revoke,token:str,...}]}，event ID唯一，按(ts,kind优先级revoke<issue<refresh,id)处理，ts>now的事件不执行但仍校验schema。issue含subject非空str、expires:int>ts、groups:list[str]；refresh含parent:str、expires:int>ts；revoke不带额外必需字段。token只能issue/refresh一次，否则ValueError。revoke可先于签发，撤销标记永久保留；刷新父令牌必须已签发、在刷新时未到期且其祖先未撤销，否则子令牌被签发但永久无效。有效refresh继承subject/groups，轮换后父令牌标为superseded且不能再次刷新；祖先之后撤销也使全部后代无效，但祖先自然到期或被轮换不使已签发子令牌失效。输出 {active_tokens:[{token,subject,groups,expires}],invalid_tokens:list[token],revoked:list[token]}；now>=expires失效，各列表按token排序，groups去重排序。缺字段、非法类型ValueError。\n39: \n40: 补充边界：校验阶段覆盖全部事件，包括未来事件，且issue/refresh的token唯一性也跨未来事件检查；未来事件不进入输出状态。撤销可针对从未签发的token，revoked仍保留该标识。只有有效refresh才将父token置superseded；无效子token仍出现在invalid_tokens，但不消耗父token的轮换机会。祖先自然过期与superseded不追溯影响已有效签发的子token；永久撤销才沿祖先链传播。三类token列表指已处理事件得到的状态，invalid_tokens不包含纯未来签发。subject/token/group身份保持原值，不统一小写。\n41: \n42: 局部检查命令：`python3 sessions/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n43: \n44: ### authorization — 组继承闭包与跨组件授权\n45: \n46: operation：`federation_authorize`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n47: \n48: 输入顺序为 {groups:[{id,parents:list[group_id],roles:list[str]}],rules:[{id,role,resource:glob,action:glob,effect:allow/deny}],requests:[{id,token,resource,action}]}、schema_compatibility结果、session_replay结果。所有ID各自唯一，未知parent ValueError；组继承允许环，计算可达闭包直到不再增长，合并角色。只承认active_tokens；token中未知组ValueError。模式不兼容时全部deny(reason=schema)，无活动token则deny(reason=session)，否则匹配角色及区分大小写fnmatchcase资源/动作；任何deny规则优先，无deny且有allow才allow，默认deny。胜出规则按同effect中ID字典序最小者。输出 {decisions:[{id,effect,reason:schema/session/rule/default,rule_id:str或null,roles:list[str]}],allow,deny}，decisions保留请求顺序，roles排序。\n49: \n50: 补充边界：schema/session前置失败时roles=[]、rule_id=null；schema失败优先于session失败。合法schema和活动token下，roles是其所有组及父组的完整角色并集，即使最终default deny也返回roles。不要套用policy_precedence的priority/specificity：本operation只在匹配规则中先deny，再按同effect的id最小选胜者。未知活动token组即使schema不兼容也属于输入错误；没有被活动token引用的请求token不报未知ID，而按session拒绝。\n51: \n52: 局部检查命令：`python3 authorization/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n53: \n54: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n55: \n56: 所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n57: `run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n58: \n59: `final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`身份联邦迁移`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n60: sha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\n61: attention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n62: \n63: ## 验收与执行边界\n64: \n65: 在工作区运行`python3 check.py`。公开检查会清理旧outputs、完成适用的材料物化、运行run_all并检查模块与输出结构；公开通过不代表全部业务语义正确。外部只读验证器另外检查受保护文件、全部预期产物、规范序列化及接口边界输入。\n66: 给Agent的材料只有本任务TASK.md和workspace。作者参考实现、评分器、审计记录、历史输出和其他任务不得作为工作区或模型上下文输入。\n\n(End of 身份联邦迁移：接口与业务规格\n\n修复缺失实现，依据真实输入生成可重建、确定性的JSON产物。以下业务算法的节点、边、窗口或资源是被实现程序的数据语义，不是Agent执行计划。\n\n## 实现范围与保护边界\n\n组件目录为`claims/`、`sessions/`、`authorization/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\n不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\n\n本题各包已有__init__.py与parser.py为稳定接口，不得修改。调用`<package>.parser.parse(payloads, contract)`必须得到与该包transform相同的完整封装；同时保留可直接导入model/transform/render的函数接口。\n\n## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n\n- `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n- `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n- 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n- 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n- contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n\n本题强化operation（federation_authorize, session_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### claims — 字段模式兼容\n\noperation：`schema_compatibility`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{old:[{name,type,required,default?}],new:[同格式]}。name去空白且非空，每个数组内规范化name唯一；type只能int/float/str/bool，required必须是布尔。default只看字段是否存在，不以truthiness判断，也不要求自动类型转换。按字段名并集升序输出changes：删除旧字段→{field,code:removed,breaking:true}；新增字段→code:added，仅required=true且没有default键时breaking=true；同名类型改变→code:type，只有int→float为非breaking；旧required=false变新true→再追加code:required，仅新字段没有default键时breaking=true。类型与required同时改变时，同一字段先type后required；required=true变false不单独产生change，单独default变化也不产生change。result={compatible:没有任何breaking 组继承闭包与跨组件授权\n\noperation：`federation_authorize`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入顺序为 {groups:[{id,parents:list[group_id],roles:list[str]}],rules:[{id,role,resource:glob,action:glob,effect:allow/deny}],requests:[{id,token,resource,action}]}、schema_compatibility结果、session_replay结果。所有ID各自唯一，未知parent ValueError；组继承允许环，计算可达闭包直到不再增长，合并角色。只承认active_tokens；token中未知组ValueError。模式不兼容时全部deny(reason=schema)，无活动token则deny(reason=session)，否则匹配角色及区分大小写fnmatchcase资源/动作；任何deny规则优先，无deny且有allow才allow，默认deny。胜出规则按同effect中ID字典序最小者。输出 {decisions:[{id,effect,reason:schema/session/rule/default,rule_id:str或null,roles:list[str]}],allow,deny}，decisions保留请求顺序，roles排序。\n\n补充边界：schema/session前置失败时roles=[]、rule_id=null；schema失败优先于session失败。合法schema和活动token下，roles是其所有组及父组的完整角色并集，即使最终default deny也返回roles。不要套用policy_precedence的priority/specificity：本operation只在匹配规则中先deny，再按同effect的id最小选胜者。未知活动token组即使schema不兼容也属于输入错误；没有被活动token引用的请求token不报未知ID，而按session拒绝。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 authorization/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n\n所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n`run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 该组件的result}}`。\n\n`final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`身份联邦迁移`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\nsha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\nattention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n\n## Let me write:\n\n```python\ndef ancestor_revoked(tok, revoked, tokens):\n    cur = tok\n    while cur is not None:\n        if cur in revoked:\n            return True\n        info = tokens.get(cur)\n        if info is None:\n            return False\n        cur = info['parent']\n    return False\n```\n\nThis walks from token up through parent chain, checking revoked set.\n\nBut wait — there's a subtlety about \"已签发\" (issued). Since components could have cycles? In this task, no cycles (claims/sessions independent, authorization depends on both). But to be safe, I'll do a stable topological sort using DFS with cycle detection → error if cycle.\n\nLet me implement a deterministic topo sort. Since I want a stable/deterministic order, I'll sort names for iteration.\n\nActually, simpler: use Kahn's algorithm with a sorted queue, or DFS. Let me do DFS postorder:\n\n```python\ndef topo_sort(info, deps):\n    visited = set()\n    temp = set()\n    order = []\n    def visit(n):\n        if n in temp:\n            raise ValueError('dependency cycle')\n        if n in visited:\n            return\n        temp.add(n)\n        for dep in sorted(deps[n]):\n            visit(dep)\n        temp.remove(n)\n        visited.add(n)\n        order.append(n)\n    for n in sorted(info):\n        visit(n)\n    return order\n```\n\nThis gives a valid topological order (dependencies first). transform(payloads, contract):\n    if len(payloads) < 1:\n        raise ValueError('schema_compatibility requires one input')\n    data = payloads[0]\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError('input must be an object')\n    if 'old' not in data or 'new' not in data:\n        raise ValueError('missing old/new fields')\n\n    old = _parse_fields(data['old'], 'old')\n    new = _parse_fields(data['new'], 'new')\n\n    names = sorted(set(old) | set(new))\n    changes = []\n    compatible = True\n    for name in names:\n        if name in old and name not in new:\n            changes.append({'field': name, 'code': 'removed', 'breaking': True})\n            compatible = False\n        elif name in new and name not in old:\n            t, req, has_default = new[name]\n            breaking = req and not has_default\n            changes.append({'field': name, 'code': 'added', 'breaking': breaking})\n            if breaking:\n                compatible = False\n changes.append({'field': name, 'code': 'type', 'breaking': breaking})\n                if breaking:\n                    compatible = False\n            if (not oreq) and nreq:\n                breaking = not ndef\n                changes.append({'field': name, 'code': 'required', 'breaking': breaking})\n                if breaking:\n                    compatible = False\n\n    return In graph theory / tree terminology, \"祖先\" (ancestors) of a node include the parent, grandparent, etc. The parent IS an ancestor. The \"descendants\" (后代) include children, grandchildren, etc. And \"祖先之后撤销也使全部后代无效\" — \"ancestor later revoked also invalidates all descendants\". Here \"后代\" (descendants) of an ancestor include the ancestor's children. So if node X is revoked, X's descendants (children, grandchildren) are invalid.\n\nFor the refresh condition \"其祖先未撤销\" (its ancestors not revoked) — \"其\" = the parent token. It aligns with \"撤销标记永久保留\".\n\nLet me finalize with `_ancestor_revoked` checking self-first (inclusive).\n\nNow, `_ancestor_revoked`:\n\n```python\ndef _ancestor_revoked(token, revoked, tokens):\n    cur = token\n    while cur is not None:\n        if cur in revoked:\n            return True\n        info = tokens.get(cur)\n        if info is None:\n            return False\n        cur = info['parent']\n    return False\n```\n\nThis walks the chain. For a token not in tokens (shouldn't happen for final state, since we only call on tokens in `tokens`), returns False. For refresh validity, parent might not be in tokens (handled by `parent_info is not None` check first, and `_ancestor_revoked` is only called in the `and` chain after parent_info is not None... actually no, I call it in the `valid = (...)` expression with short-circuit: `parent_info is not None and ... and _ancestor_revoked(parent, revoked, tokens)`. Since `parent_info is not None` is False → short circuits, `_ancestor_revoked` not called. Good. And if parent_info is not None, `_ancestor_revoked(parent)` walks from parent. parent is in tokens (parent_info is not None means parent in tokens). Good.\n\nNow `_ensure_finite`:\n\n```python\ndef _ensure_finite(obj):\n    if isinstance(obj, float):\n        if not math.isfinite(obj):\n            raise ValueError('non-finite number in payload')\n    elif isinstance(obj, dict):\n        for v in obj.values():\n            _ensure_finite(v)\n    elif isinstance(obj, list):\n        for v in obj:\n            _ensure_finite(v)\n```\n\nNow `_parse_event`:\n\n```python\ndef _parse_event(e):\n    if not isinstance(e, dict):\n        raise ValueError('event must be an object')\n    if 'id' not in e:\n        raise ValueError('event missing id')\n    eid = e['id']\n    if not isinstance(eid, str):\n        raise ValueError('event id must be str')\n    if 'ts' not in e:\n        raise ValueError('event        raise ValueError('event missing token')\n    token = e['token']\n    if not isinstance(token, str):\n        raise ValueError('event token must be str')\n    ev = {'id': eid, 'ts': ts, 'kind': kind, 'token': token}\n    if kind == 'issue':\n        if 'subject' not in e:\n            raise ValueError('issue missing subject')\n        subject = e['subject']\n        if not expires')\n        expires = e['expires']\n        if type(expires) is not int:\n            raise ValueError('expires must be int')\n        if expires <= ts:\n            raise ValueError('expires ValueError('groups must be a list')\n        for g in groups:\n            if not isinstance(g, str):\n                raise ValueError('group must be str')\n        ev['subject'] = subject\n        ev['expires'] = expires\n        ev['groups'] = groups\n    elif kind == 'refresh':\n        if 'parent' not in e:\n            raise ValueError('refresh missing parent')\n        parent =   expires = e['expires']\n        if type(expires) is not int:\n            raise ValueError('expires must be int')\n        if expires <= ts:\n            raise ValueError('expires must be > ts')\n        ev['parent'] = parent\n        ev['expires'] = expires\n    return ev\n```\n\nThis looks good.\n\nNow authorization transform.py:\n\n```python\nimport fnmatch\nimport math\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    for p in payloads:\n        _ensure_finite(p)\n    if len(payloads) < 3:\n        raise ValueError('federation_authorize requires three inputs')\n    raw = = _parse_rules(raw['rules'])\n    requests = _parse_requests(raw['requests'])\n\n    # check unknown parents\n    for gid, g in groups.items():\n        for pid in g['parents']:\n            if pid not in groups:\n                raise ValueError('unknown parent group')\n\n    # active tokens\n    active_tokens = _parse_active_tokens(sessions, groups)\n\n    compatible = claims.get('compatible') if isinstance(claims, dict) else None\n    schema_fail = compatible is False\n\n    decisions = []\n    for req in requests:\n        rid = req['id']\n        token = req['token']\n        resource = req['resource']\n        action = req['action']\n        if schema_fail:\n            decisions.append({'id': rid, 'effect': 'deny', 'reason': 'schema', 'rule_id': None, 'roles': []})\n            continue\n        if token not in active_tokens:\n            decisions.append({'id': rid, 'effect': 'deny', 'reason': 'session', 'rule_id': None, 'roles': []})\n            continue\n        roles = set()\n        for grp in active_tokens[token]:\n            roles.update(_merged_roles(grp, groups))\n        roles_sorted = sorted(roles)\n        matching_deny = []\n        matching_allow = []\n        for rule in rules:\n            if rule['role'] not in roles:\n                continue\n            if not fnmatch.fnmatchcase(resource, rule['resource']):\n                continue\n            if not fnmatch.fnmatchcase(action, rule['action']):\n                continue\n            if rule['effect'] == 'deny':\n                matching_deny.append(rule['id'])\n            else:\n                matching_allow.append(rule['id'])\n        if matching_deny:\n            decisions.append({'id': rid, 'effect': 'deny', 'reason': 'rule', 'rule_id': min(matching_deny), 'roles': roles_sorted})\n        elif matching_allow:\n            decisions.append({'id': rid, 'effect': 'allow', 'reason': 'rule', 'rule_id': min(matching_allow), 'roles': roles_sorted})\n        else:\n            decisions.append({'id': rid, 'effect': 'deny', 'reason': 'default', 'rule_id': None, 'roles': roles_sorted})\n\n    allow = sum(1 for d in decisions if d['effect'] == 'allow')\n    deny = sum(1 for d in decisions if d['effect'] == 'deny')\n\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': {'decisions': decisions, 'allow': allow, 'deny': deny}}\n```\n\nHelpers:\n\n```python\ndef _unwrap(obj):\n    if isinstance(obj, dict) and set(obj.keys()) == {'name', 'result'}:\n        return obj['result']\n    return obj\n\ndef _ensure_finite(obj): ...\n\ndef _parse_groups(arr):\n    if not isinstance(arr, isinstance(r, dict):\n            raise ValueError('rule must be an object')\n        if 'id' not in r:\n            raise ValueError('rule missing id')\n        rid = r['id']\n        if not isinstance(rid, str):\n            raise ValueError('rule id must be str')\n        if rid in seen:\n            raise ValueError('duplicate rule id')\n        seen.add(rid)\n        if 'role' not in      if 'action' not in r:\n            raise ValueError('rule missing action')\n        action = r['action']\n        if not isinstance(action, str):\n            raise ValueError('rule action must ValueError('invalid effect')\n        rules.append({'id': rid, 'role': role, 'resource': resource, 'action': action, 'effect': effect})\n    return rules\n\ndef _parse_requests(arr):\n    if not isinstance(arr, list):\n        raise ValueError('requests must be a list')\n    requests = []\n    seen = set()\n    for r in arr:\n        if not isinstance(r, dict):\n            raise ValueError('request must be an object')\n        if 'id' not in r:\n            raise ValueError('request missing id')\n        rid = r['id']\n        if not isinstance(rid, str):\n            raise ValueError('request id must be str')\n        if rid in seen:\n            raise ValueError('duplicate request id')\n        seen.add(rid)\n        for field in ('token', 'resource', 'action'):\n            if field not in r:\n                raise ValueError('request missing ' + field)\n            if not isinstance(r[field], str):\n                raise ValueError('request ' + field + ' must be str')\n        requests.append({'id': rid, 'token': r['token'], 'resource': r['resource'], 'action': r['action']})\n    return requests\n\ndef _merged_roles(gid, groups):\n    seen = set()\n    stack = [gid]\n    roles = set()\n    while stack:\n        cur = stack.pop()\n        if cur in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add(cur)\n        roles.update(groups[cur]['roles'])\n        for p in groups[cur]['parents']:\n            if p not in seen:\n                stack.append(p)\n    return roles\n\ndef _parse_active_tokens(sessions, groups):\n    if not isinstance(sessions, dict):\n        raise ValueError('sessions result must be an object')\n    active_list = sessions.get('active_tokens')\n    if active_list is None:\n        raise ValueError('sessions missing active_tokens')\n groups_list = t.get('groups')\n        if not isinstance(groups_list, list):\n            raise ValueError('active token groups must be a list')\n        for g in groups_list:\n            if not isinstance(g, str):\n                raise ValueError('active token group must be str')\n            if g not in groups:\n                raise ValueError('unknown group in active token')\n        active[token] = groups_list\n    return active\n```\n\nThis looks comprehensive. Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n\n\ndef find_contracts():\n    return sorted(\n        p for p in ROOT.rglob('contract.json')\n        if 'outputs' not in p.relative_to(ROOT).parts\n    )\n\n\ndef topological_order():\n    contracts = find_contracts()\n    names = []\n    info = {}\n    for cpath in contracts:\n        c = json.loads(cpath.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n        name = c['name']\n        names.append(name)\n        info[name] = {'path': cpath, 'output': c['output'], 'inputs': list(c['inputs'])}\n    deps = {n: [] for n in names}\n    for n in names:\n        for inp in info[n]['inputs']:\n            for other in names:\n                if other != n and inp == info[other]['output']:\n                    deps[n].append(other)\n    visited = set()\n    temp = set()\n    order = []\n\n    def visit(n):\n        if n in temp:\n            raise ValueError('dependency cycle among components')\n        if n in visited:\n            return\n        temp.add(n)\n        for d in sorted(deps[n]):\n            visit(d)\n        temp.remove(n)\n        visited.add(n)\n        order.append(n)\n\n    for n in sorted(names):\n        visit(n)\n    return order, info\n\n\ndef main():\n    order, info = topological_order()\n    for name in order:\n        cpath = info[name]['path']\n        script = cpath.with_name('render.py')\n        subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(script)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'integrate.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'final_report.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\nNow final_report.py:\n\n```python\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import non-empty; unique per array after strip.\n- `type` ∈ {int,float,str,bool}; `required` bool; `default` presence only.\n- Changes sorted by field-name union: removed breaking true; added breaking only if `required` true and no `default`; type change breaking except `int→float`; required `false→true` appends `required` with breaking if new has no `default`.\n- Output result: `{\"compatible\": bool, \"changes\": [{field,code,breaking}]}`.\n\n### sessions algorithm (`session_replay`)\n- Input `{now:int>=0, events:[...]}`.\n- Event sort order: `(ts, kind_priority, id)` with revoke=0, issue=1, refresh=2.\n- Validate all events including future; execute only `ts <= now`.\n- issue/refresh `token` uniqueness across all events; event `id` uniqueness.\n- issue requires non-empty-after-strip subject str, `expires:int>ts`, `groups:list[str]`.\n- refresh requires parent str, `expires:int>ts`.\n- revoke requires no extra required fields.\n- Refresh validity: parent exists in tokens, `parent.expires > refresh.ts`, parent not superseded, parent/ancestor chain not revoked; only valid refresh marks parent superseded.\n- Output result: `{\"active_tokens\":[{\"token\",\"subject\",\"groups\",\"expires\"}], \"invalid_tokens\":[token,...], \"revoked\":[token,...]}`; lists sorted; groups dedup+sorted.\n\n### authorization algorithm (`federation_authorize`)\n- Payloads: raw `{groups,rules,requests}`, claims wrapper, sessions wrapper; unwrap last two.\n- Unique IDs per collection; unknown group parent → `ValueError`.\n- Transitive group-parent closure (cycles allowed); merged role union sorted.\n- Unknown group in any sessions active token → `ValueError` even if schema incompatible.\n- If claims `compatible` is `False` → all decisions deny reason=schema, roles=[], rule_id=null.\n- Else token not active → deny reason=session, roles=[], rule_id=null.\n- Match: rule role in token's role union + `fnmatch.fnmatchcase` resource/action (case-sensitive); any deny wins, else allow, else default deny.\n- Winning rule_id: lexicographically smallest among matching rules of same effect.\n- Output result: `{\"decisions\":[{id,effect,reason:schema/session/rule/default,rule_id,roles}], \"allow\":int, \"deny\":int}`; decisions preserve request order; roles sorted.\n\n### Expected example outputs\n- claims: `{\"compatible\": true, \"changes\": [{\"field\":\"tenant\",\"code\":\"added\",\"breaking\":false}]}`\n- sessions: active_tokens = `new`(alice,[engineering],50), `service`(svc,[service],40); # 身份联邦迁移：接口与业务规格\n2: \n3: 修复缺失实现，依据真实输入生成可重建、确定性的JSON产物。以下业务算法的节点、边、窗口或资源是被实现程序的数据语义，不是Agent执行计划。\n4: \n5: ## 实现范围与保护边界\n6: \n7: 组件目录为`claims/`、`sessions/`、`authorization/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\n8: 不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\n9: \n10: 本题各包已有__init__.py与parser.py为稳定接口，不得修改。调用`<package>.parser.parse(payloads, contract)`必须得到与该包transform相同的完整封装；同时保留可直接导入model/transform/render的函数接口。\n11: \n12: ## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n13: \n14: - `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n15: - `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n16: - 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n17: - `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n18: - int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n19: - 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n20: - contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n21: \n22: 本题强化operation（federation_authorize, session_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n23: \n24: ## 各组件的完整业务规则\n25: \n26: ### claims — 字段模式兼容\n27: \n28: operation：`schema_compatibility`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n29: \n30: 首输入{old:[{name,type,required,default?}],new:[同格式]}。name去空白且非空，每个数组内规范化name唯一；type只能int/float/str/bool，required必须是布尔。default只看字段是否存在，不以truthiness判断，也不要求自动类型转换。按字段名并集升序输出changes：删除旧字段→{field,code:removed,breaking:true}；新增字段→code:added，仅required=true且没有default键时breaking=true；同名类型改变→code:type，只有int→float为非breaking；旧required=false变新true→再追加code:required，仅新字段没有default键时breaking=true。类型与required同时改变时，同一字段先type后required；required=true变false不单独产生change，单独default变化也不产生change。result={compatible:没有任何breaking change,changes}。空old/new为true和[]；不返回records或组件依赖图。\n31: \n32: 局部检查命令：`python3 claims/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n33: \n34: ### sessions — 乱序令牌轮换及传递撤销\n35: \n36: operation：`session_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n37: \n38: 输入 {now:int>=0,events:[{id,ts:int>=0,kind:issue/refresh/revoke,token:str,...}]}，event ID唯一，按(ts,kind优先级revoke<issue<refresh,id)处理，ts>now的事件不执行但仍校验schema。issue含subject非空str、expires:int>ts、groups:list[str]；refresh含parent:str、expires:int>ts；revoke不带额外必需字段。token只能issue/refresh一次，否则ValueError。revoke可先于签发，撤销标记永久保留；刷新父令牌必须已签发、在刷新时未到期且其祖先未撤销，否则子令牌被签发但永久无效。有效refresh继承subject/groups，轮换后父令牌标为superseded且不能再次刷新；祖先之后撤销也使全部后代无效，但祖先自然到期或被轮换不使已签发子令牌失效。输出 {active_tokens:[{token,subject,groups,expires}],invalid_tokens:list[token],revoked:list[token]}；now>=expires失效，各列表按token排序，groups去重排序。缺字段、非法类型ValueError。\n39: \n40: 补充边界：校验阶段覆盖全部事件，包括未来事件，且issue/refresh的token唯一性也跨未来事件检查；未来事件不进入输出状态。撤销可针对从未签发的token，revoked仍保留该标识。只有有效refresh才将父token置superseded；无效子token仍出现在invalid_tokens，但不消耗父token的轮换机会。祖先自然过期与superseded不追溯影响已有效签发的子token；永久撤销才沿祖先链传播。三类token列表指已处理事件得到的状态，invalid_tokens不包含纯未来签发。subject/token/group身份保持原值，不统一小写。\n41: \n42: 局部检查命令：`python3 sessions/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n43: \n44: ### authorization — 组继承闭包与跨组件授权\n45: \n46: operation：`federation_authorize`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n47: \n48: 输入顺序为 {groups:[{id,parents:list[group_id],roles:list[str]}],rules:[{id,role,resource:glob,action:glob,effect:allow/deny}],requests:[{id,token,resource,action}]}、schema_compatibility结果、session_replay结果。所有ID各自唯一，未知parent ValueError；组继承允许环，计算可达闭包直到不再增长，合并角色。只承认active_tokens；token中未知组ValueError。模式不兼容时全部deny(reason=schema)，无活动token则deny(reason=session)，否则匹配角色及区分大小写fnmatchcase资源/动作；任何deny规则优先，无deny且有allow才allow，默认deny。胜出规则按同effect中ID字典序最小者。输出 {decisions:[{id,effect,reason:schema/session/rule/default,rule_id:str或null,roles:list[str]}],allow,deny}，decisions保留请求顺序，roles排序。\n49: \n50: 补充边界：schema/session前置失败时roles=[]、rule_id=null；schema失败优先于session失败。合法schema和活动token下，roles是其所有组及父组的完整角色并集，即使最终default deny也返回roles。不要套用policy_precedence的priority/specificity：本operation只在匹配规则中先deny，再按同effect的id最小选胜者。未知活动token组即使schema不兼容也属于输入错误；没有被活动token引用的请求token不报未知ID，而按session拒绝。\n51: \n52: 局部检查命令：`python3 authorization/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n53: \n54: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n55: \n56: 所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n57: 身份联邦迁移：接口与业务规格\n\n修复缺失实现，依据真实输入生成可重建、确定性的JSON产物。以下业务算法的节点、边、窗口或资源是被实现程序的数据语义，不是Agent执行计划。\n\n## 实现范围与保护边界\n\n组件目录为`claims/`、`sessions/`、`authorization/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\n不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\n\n本题各包已有__init__.py与parser.py为稳定接口，不得修改。调用`<package>.parser.parse(payloads, contract)`必须得到与该包transform相同的完整封装；同时保留可直接导入model/transform/render的函数接口。\n\n## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n\n- `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n- `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n- 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n- 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n- contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n\n本题强化operation（federation_authorize, session_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### claims — 字段模式兼容\n\noperation：`schema_compatibility`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{old:[{name,type,required,default?}],new:[同格式]}。name去空白且非空，每个数组内规范化name唯一；type只能int/float/str/bool，required必须是布尔。default只看字段是否存在，不以truthiness判断，也不要求自动类型转换。按字段名并集升序输出changes：删除旧字段→{field,code:removed,breaking:true}；新增字段→code:added，仅required=true且没有default键时breaking=true；同名类型改变→code:type，只有int→float为非breaking；旧required=false变新true→再追加code:required，仅新字段没有default键时breaking=true。类型与required同时改变时，同一字段先type后required；required=true变false不单独产生change，单独default变化也不产生change。result={compatible:没有任何breaking change,changes}。空old/new为true和[]；不返回records或组件依赖图。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 claims/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### sessions — 乱序令牌轮换及传递撤销\n\noperation：`session_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入 {now:int>=0,events:[{id,ts:int>=0,kind:issue/refresh/revoke,token:str,...}]}，event ID唯一，按(ts,kind优先级revoke<issue<refresh,id)处理，ts>now的事件不执行但仍校验schema。issue含subject非空str、expires:int>ts、groups:list[str]；refresh含parent:str、expires:int>ts；revoke不带额外必需字段。token只能issue/refresh一次，否则ValueError。revoke可先于签发，撤销标记永久保留；刷新父令牌必须已签发、在刷新时未到期且其祖先未撤销，否则子令牌被签发但永久无效。有效refresh继承subject/groups，轮换后父令牌标为superseded且不能再次刷新；祖先之后撤销也使全部后代无效，但祖先自然到期或被轮换不使已签发子令牌失效。输出 {active_tokens:[{token,subject,groups,expires}],invalid_tokens:list[token],revoked:list[token]}；now>=expires失效，各列表按token排序，groups去重排序。缺字段、非法类型ValueError。\n\n补充边界：校验阶段覆盖全部事件，包括未来事件，且issue/refresh的token唯一性也跨未来事件检查；未来事件不进入输出状态。撤销可针对从未签发的token，revoked仍保留该标识。只有有效refresh才将父token置superseded；无效子token仍出现在invalid_tokens，但不消耗父token的轮换机会。祖先自然过期与superseded不追溯影响已有效签发的子token；永久撤销才沿祖先链传播。三类token列表指已处理事件得到的状态，invalid_tokens不包含纯未来签发。subject/token/group身份保持原值，不统一小写。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 sessions/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### authorization — 组继承闭包与跨组件授权\n\noperation：`federation_authorize`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入顺序为 {groups:[{id,parents:list[group_id],roles:list[str]}],rules:[{id,role,resource:glob,action:glob,effect:allow/deny}],requests:[{id,token,resource,action}]}、schema_compatibility结果、session_replay结果。所有ID各自唯一，未知parent ValueError；组继承允许环，计算可达闭包直到不再增长，合并角色。只承认active_tokens；token中未知组ValueError。模式不兼容时全部deny(reason=schema)，无活动token则deny(reason=session)，否则匹配角色及区分大小写fnmatchcase资源/动作；任何deny规则优先，无deny且有allow才allow，默认deny。胜出规则按同effect中ID字典序最小者。输出 {decisions:[{id,effect,reason:schema/session/rule/default,rule_id:str或null,roles:list[str]}],allow,deny}，decisions保留请求顺序，roles排序。\n\n补充边界：schema/session前置失败时roles=[]、rule_id=null；schema失败优先于session失败。合法schema和活动token下，roles是其所有组及父组的完整角色并集，即使最终default deny也返回roles。不要套用policy_precedence的priority/specificity：本operation只在匹配规则中先deny，再按同effect的id最小选胜者。未知活动token组即使schema不兼容也属于输入错误；没有被活动token引用的请求token不报未知ID，而按session拒绝。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 authorization/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n\n所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n`run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 Now let me also handle the \"finite\" check for sessions: recursively check all payloads for NaN/Infinity.\n\nNow, the token map. Let me write the algorithm:\n\n```python\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    data = payloads[0]\n    _ensure_finite(data)  # recursive finite check\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError('sessions input must be an object')\n    now = data.get('now')\n    if type(now) is not int or now < 0:\n        raise ValueError('now must be int >= 0')\n    events = data.get('events')\n    if not isinstance(events, list):\n        raise ValueError('events must be a list')\n    \n    # validate schema, collect events\n    parsed = []\n    seen_ids = set()\n    token_owners = {}  # token -> kind (issue/refresh), for uniqueness\n    for e in events:\n        parsed_event = _parse_event(e, seen_ids, token_owners)\n        parsed.append(parsed_event)\n    \n    # sort by (ts, kind_priority, id)\n    parsed.sort(key=lambda x: (x['ts'], _kind_priority(x['kind']), x['id']))\n    \n    # process events with ts <= now\n    tokens = {}  # token -> dict\n    superseded = set()\n    revoked = set()\n    invalid = set()  # permanently invalid tokens\n    \n    for e in parsed:\n        if e['ts'] > now:\n            continue  # not executed\n        kind = e['kind']\n        if kind == 'issue':\n            tokens[e['token']] = {\n                'subject': e['subject'],\n                'groups': e['groups'],\n                'expires': e['expires'],\n                'ts': e['ts'],\n                'parent': None,\n            }\n        elif kind == 'refresh':\n            parent = e['parent']\n            # validity check\n            valid = _is_valid_refresh(parent, tokens, superseded, revoked, e['ts'])\n            if valid:\n                ptok = tokens[parent]\n                tokens[e['token']] = {\n                    'subject': ptok['subject'],\n                    'groups': ptok['groups'],\n                    'expires': e['expires'],\n                    'ts': e['ts'],\n                    'parent': parent,\n                }\n                superseded.add(parent)\n            else:\n                tokens[e['token']] = {\n                    'subject': None,\n                    'groups': [],\n                    'expires': e['expires'],\n                    'ts': e['ts'],\n                    'parent': parent,\n                }\n                invalid.add(e['token'])\n        elif kind == 'revoke':\n            revoked.add(e['token'])\n    \n    # compute active/invalid lists\n    active = []\n    invalid_tokens = []\n    issued_tokens = tokens  # only issued (ts<=now)\n    for token, t in issued_tokens.items():\n        if _is_active(token, t, superseded, revoked, invalid, now):\n            active.append({'token': token, 'subject': t['subject'], 'groups': sorted(set(t['groups'])), 'expires': t['expires']})\n        else:\n            invalid_tokens.append(token)\n    \n    active.sort(key=lambda x: x['token'])\n    invalid_tokens.sort()\n    revoked_list = sorted(revoked)\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': {'active_tokens': active, 'invalid_tokens': invalid_tokens, 'revoked': revoked_list}}\n```\n\nNow `_is_valid_refresh(parent, tokens, superseded, revoked, ts)`:\n- parent must be in tokens (issued). If not → False.\n- parent.expires > ts (not expired at refresh time). If not → False.\n- parent not superseded. If superseded → False.\n- parent's ancestor chain not revoked (including parent). If revoked → False.\n\nWait, \"其祖先未撤销\" — ancestors not revoked. Does it include the parent? The revoke of an ancestor happens at some ts. If the ancestor is revoked at ts > refresh.ts (later), does that invalidate the refresh? \"刷新父令牌必须...其祖先未撤销\" — at refresh time, ancestor not revoked. So if ancestor is revoked LATER (after refresh), the refresh was valid at the time, but then \"祖先之后撤销也使全部后代无效\" — the child becomes invalid later.\n\nSo in my processing (time-ordered), at the time of refresh, `revoked` set only contains tokens revoked up to that point (ts <= refresh.ts). Let me write:\n\n```python\ndef _parse_event(e, seen_ids, token_owners):\n    if not isinstance(e, dict):\n        raise ValueError('event must be object')\n    eid = e.get('id')\n    if not isinstance(eid, str):\n        raise ValueError('event id must be str')\n    if eid in seen_ids:\n        raise ValueError('duplicate event id')\n    seen_ids.add(eid)\n    ts = e.get('ts')\n    if type(ts) is not int or ts < 0:\n        raise ValueError('ts must be int >= 0')\n    kind = e.get('kind')\n    if kind not in ('issue','refresh','revoke'):\n        raise ValueError('invalid kind')\n    token = e.get('token')\n    if not isinstance(token, str):\n        raise ValueError('token must be str')\n    out = {'id': eid, 'ts': ts, 'kind': kind, 'token': token}\n    if kind == 'issue':\n        subject = e.get('subject')\n        if not isinstance(subject, str) or subject.strip() == '':\n            raise ValueError('subject must be non-empty str')\n        expires = e.get('expires')\n        if type(expires) is not int or expires <= ts:\n            raise ValueError('expires must be int > ts')\n        groups = e.get('groups')\n        if not isinstance(groups, list) or any(not isinstance(g, str) for g in groups):\n            raise ValueError('groups must be list[str]')\n        out['subject'] = subject\n        out['expires'] = expires\n        out['groups'] = list(groups)\n        _register_token(token, kind, token_owners)\n    elif kind == 'refresh':\n        parent = e.get('parent')\n        if not isinstance(parent, str):\n            raise ValueError('parent must be str')\n        expires = e.get('expires')\n        if type(expires) is not int or expires <= ts:\n            raise ValueError('expires must be int > ts')\n        out['parent'] = parent\n        out['expires'] = expires\n        _register_token(token, kind, token_owners)\n    # revoke: nothing extra\n    return out\n\ndef _register_token(token, kind, token_owners):\n    if token in token_owners:\n        raise ValueError('token issued/refreshed more than once')\n    token_owners[token] = kind\n```\n\nWait, for `expires` type: `type(expires) is not int` — this excludes bool (True is not int by type()). Yes.\n    - So read rule matches (role reader in roles, resource matches, action read matches). allow.\n    - write: role developer, resource repo/*, action write. action \"read\" != \"write\". So not schema failure.\n\nGreat.\n\nNow let me carefully write the authorization validation and closure.\n\n```python\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    for p in payloads:\n        _ensure_finite(p)\n    raw = payloads[0]\n    claims = _unwrap(payloads[1])\n    sessions = _unwrap(payloads[2])\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError('authorization input must be object')\n    # groups\n    groups = _parse_groups(raw.get('groups'))\n    rules = _parse_rules(raw.get('rules'))\n    requests = _parse_requests(raw.get('requests'))\n    # group id set\n    group_ids = set(groups)\n    # validate parents (unknown parent)\n    for g in groups.values():\n        for p in g['parents']:\n            if p not in group_ids:\n                raise ValueError('unknown parent group: ' + p)\n    # compute closure roles\n    closure_roles = _compute_closure_roles(groups)\n    # parse claims compatible\n    # claims result: {compatible, changes}\n    if not isinstance(claims, dict) or 'compatible' not in claims:\n        raise ValueError('claims result missing compatible')\n    compatible = claims['compatible']\n    if not isinstance(compatible, bool):\n        raise ValueError('compatible must be bool')\n    # parse sessions active tokens\n    if not isinstance(sessions, dict) or 'active_tokens' not in sessions:\n        raise ValueError('sessions result missing active_tokens')\n    active_tokens = sessions['active_tokens']\n    if not isinstance(active_tokens, list):\n        raise ValueError('active_tokens must be list')\n    active_map = {}\n    for t in active_tokens:\n        if not isinstance(t, dict):\n            raise ValueError('active token must be object')\n        token = t.get('token')\n        if not isinstance(token, str):\n            raise ValueError('token must be str')\n        tg = t.get('groups')\n        if not isinstance(tg, list) or any(not isinstance(x, str) for x in tg):\n            raise ValueError('token groups must be list[str]')\n        active_map[token] = tg\n        # unknown group check (even if schema incompatible)\n        for grp in tg:\n            if grp not in group_ids:\n                raise ValueError('unknown token group: ' + grp)\n    # now process requests\n    decisions = []\n    allow = 0\n    deny = 0\n    for r in requests:\n        if not compatible:\n            decisions.append({'id': r['id'], 'effect': 'deny', 'reason': 'schema', 'rule_id': None, 'roles': []})\n            deny += 1\n            continue\n        token = r['token']\n        if token not in active_map:\n            decisions.append({'id': r['id'], 'effect': 'deny', 'reason': 'session', 'rule_id': None, 'roles': []})\n            deny += 1\n            continue\n        token_groups = active_map[token]\n        roles = sorted(set().union(*[closure_roles[g] for g in token_groups]))\n        matches = []\n        for rule in rules:\n            if rule['role'] in roles and fnmatch.fnmatchcase(r['resource'], rule['resource']) and fnmatch.fnmatchcase(r['action'], rule['action']):\n                matches.append(rule)\n        denies = [m for m in matches if m['effect'] == 'deny']\n        allows = [m for m in matches if m['effect'] == 'allow']\n        if denies:\n            rule_id = min(m['id'] for m in denies)\n            decisions.append({'id': r['id'], 'effect': 'deny', 'reason': 'rule', 'rule_id': rule_id, 'roles': roles})\n            deny += 1\n        elif allows:\n            rule_id = min(m['id'] for m in allows)\n            decisions.append({'id': r['id'], 'effect': 'allow', 'reason': 'rule', 'rule_id': rule_id, 'roles': roles})\n            allow += 1\n        else:\n            decisions.append({'id': r['id'], 'effect': 'deny', 'reason': 'default', 'rule_id': None, 'roles': roles})\n            deny += 1\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': {'decisions': decisions, 'allow': allow, 'deny': deny}}\n```\n\nWait, roles for default deny: \"即使最终default deny也返回roles\". So roles should be the full union even for default deny. ✓ (I set roles = full union).\n\nNow `_compute_closure_roles`:\n```python\ndef _compute_closure_roles(groups):\n    result = {}\n    for gid, g in groups.items():\n        reachable = set([gid])\n        # BFS through parents\n        stack = list(g['parents'])\n        while stack:\n            p = stack.pop()\n            if p in reachable:\n                continue\n            reachable.add(p)\n            stack.extend(groups[p]['parents'])\n        roles = set()\n        for rid in reachable:\n            roles.update(groups[rid]['roles'])\n        result[gid] = roles\n    return result\n```\n\nWait, this BFS computes transitive closure correctly with cycle handling (using `reachable` visited set). For engineering: reachable starts {engineering}, stack [staff]. pop staff, add staff, stack.extend(staff.parents=[engineering]). pop engineering, already in reachable, skip. raise ValueError('invalid effect')\n        result.append({'id': rid, 'role': role, 'resource': resource, 'action': action, 'effect': effect})\n    return result\n```\n\n`_parse_requests`:\n```python\ndef _parse_requests(requests):\n    if not isinstance(requests, list):\n        raise ValueError('requests must be list')\n    result = []\n    seen = dependency: for each contract, its inputs may reference outputs of other components\n    # outputs are like \"outputs/claims.json\"\n    deps = {}\n    for name, (p, c) in contracts_by_name.items():\n        deps[name] = []\n        for inp in c['inputs']:\n            # if input path matches another component's output, add dependency\n            for other_name, (op, oc) in contracts_by_name.items():\n                if other_name != name and inp == oc['output']:\n                    deps[name].append(other_name)\n    # topological sort (DFS)\n    visited = {}\n    order = []\n    def visit(name):\n        if visited.get(name) == 1:\n            raise ValueError('cycle in dependencies')\n        if visited.get(name) == 2:\n            return\n        visited[name] = 1\n        for d in sorted(deps[name]):\n            visit(d)\n        visited[name] = 2\n        order.append(name)\n    for name in sorted(contracts_by_name):\n        visit(name)\n    return [contracts_by_name[n] for n in order], deps\n\ndef main():\n    contracts_list, deps = topological_order()\n    for path, contract in contracts_list:\n        render = path.with_name('render.py')\n        subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(render)], cwd=ROOT, check=True, timeout=30)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT/'integrate.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True, timeout=30)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT/'final_report.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True, timeout=30)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\nWait, the dependency detection: a component's input that equals another component's output means dependency.
